    "view_def": " SELECT l.seller_id,\n    count(l.id) AS total_listings,\n    count(\n        CASE\n            WHEN l.is_published = true THEN 1\n            ELSE NULL::integer\n        END) AS published_listings,\n    count(\n        CASE\n            WHEN l.boost_priority > 0 AND l.is_published = true THEN 1\n            ELSE NULL::integer\n        END) AS boosted_listings,\n    sum(l.views_count) AS total_views,\n    avg(l.price) AS average_price,\n    max(l.created_at) AS last_listing_date\n   FROM listings l\n  GROUP BY l.seller_id;"
  }
]

The trigger will handle the rest.\r\n  UPDATE public.active_boosts\r\n  SET status = 'expired'\r\n  WHERE status = 'active' AND expires_at < now();\r\nEND;\r\n$function$\n"

    "schema": "public",
    "routine_name": "increment_listing_views",
    "function_def": "CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.increment_listing_views(listing_id uuid)\n RETURNS void\n LANGUAGE plpgsql\n SECURITY DEFINER\nAS $function$\r\nBEGIN\r\n    UPDATE public.listings \r\n    SET views_count = views_count + 1 \r\n    WHERE id = listing_id AND is_published = true;\r\nEND;\r\n$function$\n"
  },
  {
    "schema": "public",
    "routine_name": "sync_listing_boost_status",
    "function_def": "CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.sync_listing_boost_status()\n RETURNS trigger\n LANGUAGE plpgsql\n SECURITY DEFINER\nAS $function$\r\nDECLARE\r\n  v_priority_level INT;\r\nBEGIN\r\n  -- If a boost becomes ACTIVE\r\n  IF (TG_OP = 'INSERT' OR TG_OP = 'UPDATE') AND NEW.status = 'active' THEN\r\n    -- Get the priority from the plan\r\n    SELECT priority_level\r\n    INTO v_priority_level\r\n    FROM public.boost_plans\r\n    WHERE id = NEW.plan_id;\r\n\r\n    -- Update the corresponding listing\r\n    UPDATE public.listings\r\n    SET \r\n      boost_priority = v_priority_level,\r\n      boost_expires_at = NEW.expires_at\r\n    WHERE id = NEW.listing_id;\r\n\r\n  -- If a boost becomes EXPIRED or CANCELLED (from an active state)\r\n  ELSIF TG_OP = 'UPDATE' AND NEW.status IN ('expired', 'cancelled') AND OLD.status = 'active' THEN\r\n    -- Reset the listing's boost status\r\n    UPDATE public.listings\r\n    SET \r\n      boost_priority = 0,\r\n      boost_expires_at = NULL\r\n    WHERE id = NEW.listing_id;\r\n  END IF;\r\n\r\n  RETURN NEW;\r\nEND;\r\n$function$\n"
  },
  {
    "schema": "public",
    "routine_name": "update_booking_statuses",
    "function_def": "CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.update_booking_statuses()\n RETURNS void\n LANGUAGE plpgsql\nAS $function$\r\nBEGIN\r\n  -- 1. Decline expired pending requests\r\n  UPDATE booking_requests\r\n  SET status = 'declined',\r\n      updated_at = NOW()\r\n  WHERE status = 'pending'\r\n    AND (\r\n      (TO_TIMESTAMP(event_date || ' ' || split_part(event_time, '-', 2), 'YYYY-MM-DD HH12:MI AM') AT TIME ZONE 'America/Denver')\r\n      <= NOW()\r\n    );\r\n\r\n  -- 2. Complete expired accepted requests\r\n  UPDATE booking_requests\r\n  SET status = 'completed',\r\n      updated_at = NOW()\r\n  WHERE status = 'accepted'\r\n    AND (\r\n      (TO_TIMESTAMP(event_date || ' ' || split_part(event_time, '-', 2), 'YYYY-MM-DD HH12:MI AM') AT TIME ZONE 'America/Denver')\r\n      <= NOW()\r\n    );\r\nEND;\r\n$function$\n"
  }
]

g5eventli@cloudshell:~ (eventli-webapp)$ # Loop through each secret in the project
for SECRET in $(gcloud secrets list --format="value(name)"); do

  # Print a header for the secret
  echo "--- Secret: $SECRET ---"

  # Access and print the latest version's value
  gcloud secrets versions access latest --secret="$SECRET"

  # Add a newline for better readability
  echo -e "\n"

done
